- work with w = a / sum(a) (sum1), length n.
- compute c = conv(w,w) (sum coefficients =1). Objective raw = max(c). score=2n*max(c).
- To reduce max, use a penalty: P = ∑ g(c_s) where g(x)=max(0, x - T)^2 + λ max(0,x-T), with T current max or target. Gradient = 2*conv(w, g'(c)) (or correlation). Because c_s=w*w, derivative of ∑φ(c_s) wrt w_j = 2 ∑_s φ'(c_s) w_{s-j} (valid). This is 2 * (w * φ'(c))_j but convolution orientation: (w * φ')_j =∑_i w_i φ'_{j-i}. We need ∑_s φ'_s w_{s-j}. Good. p1: s=1 h1 p0 + s=2 h2 p1. So L_h(p)_j =∑_{s} h_s p_{s-j} (with p index valid). This is not a standard convolution but can be computed by FFT if we define an array h_ext and use correlation. L_h(p)_j = ∑_i h_{i+j} p_i where i=0..n-1 and i+j< M. This is cross-correlation (with h not reversed). In FFT, cross-correlation can be computed as ifft(fft(p) * conj(fft(h))) centered. Since h length M=2n-1. We can implement: Fp=fft(p, N); Fh=fft(h, N); corr = ifft(Fp * conj(Fh)); Take appropriate real part shifted. Cross-correlation definition (p ⋆ h)_j = ∑_i p_i h_{i+j}. In numpy, correlate(p, h, mode='full')? np.correlate(p, h, 'full') returns ∑ p[n+k] h[n]? Need test mentally. But can avoid gradient and use subgradient of max only: for active lag s*, p_j contributes to c_{s*} with 2 p_{s*-j}. We can update using multiplicative for active lag(s) without full gradient. This is simpler and O(n) per active lag, no FFT gradient. We can choose multiple active lags to flatten.

For a single active lag s, update p_j *= exp(-η * p_{s-j}) for j such that s-j valid. This reduces c_s. For penalty over all lags, we could update p_j *= exp(-η * ∑_s φ'(c_s) p_{s-j}), which requires L_h; can compute via FFT correlation or maybe by looping over top lags.

We can use top-K lags: compute c, get K largest lags. For each active lag, accumulate subgradient g_j += weight_s * p_{s-j}. Then multiplicative update p *= exp(-η g / maxg) and normalize. This is O(K n) plus FFT for c. K maybe 50. For n=6000, K=50 ->300k per iter; okay for thousands. Weights can be softmax over excess c_s-T or rank. This can flatten multiple peaks.

But multiplicative update with active lags can cause oscillations. Use small η, line search: compute score after update; if not better, reduce η. For global improvement, maybe use momentum? Keep best.

We can also use additive projected gradient: p_j -= η*g_j, clip, normalize. Multiplicative preserves nonneg.

Let's design the search time budget:
- 5% time for candidates and low-dim optimization.
- Remaining for full refinement from best candidate, with periodic random restarts.

Full active-lag refinement:
Top-level `refine_active(w, n, size, rng, time_limit, tag, verbose)`.
Pseudo:
```
w=w.copy(); S=1
best= w.copy(); best_score=score(w)
eta=0.2
iters=0
stall=0
while time < limit:
  c=fft_conv(w)
  maxc=c.max()
  score=2n*maxc
  if score<best_score: best=w; best_score=score; stall=0; else stall+=1
  # choose active lags
  # use top lags by c; maybe first sort indices? M=16383, sorting each iter O(M logM) may cost. Use np.argpartition for K=200 top.
  K=min(M, 200)
  idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
  # weights: softmax((c[idx]-maxc*0.5)/maxc)
  g=np.zeros(n)
  for s in idx:
     weight = max(0, c[s] - 0.5*maxc)
     if weight==0: continue
     add contribution weight * w_slice
  gm=g.max()
  if gm>0:
     for eta_try in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25]:
        candidate = w*np.exp(-eta_try*g/gm)
        candidate *= 1/sum
        sc=score_fft(candidate)
        if sc<score: w=candidate; eta=min(2, eta*1.1); break
        else: eta=max(0.01, eta*0.7)
  # every 100 iters or stall increase: perturb
  if stall>1000 or rng.random()<0.01:
      w = perturb(w, rng, sigma=0.2)
  # accelerate if no progress: use more aggressive eta or spectral
  iters+=1
```
But scoring candidate requires FFT each time. Too expensive if multiple. We can accept first eta without line search? May hurt. Use one candidate with adaptive eta: If new score < best_score*1.05 accept? We can update best only when improvement. For speed, use `score_candidate` via FFT. One per iteration.
Active contribution loops over K up to 200. For n=8192, 1.6M ops per iter in Python loops over slices? Vectorized slicing but loop 200; okay maybe 0.01s/iter, 100k iters in 1800s? maybe 10k.
Could vectorize L_h via FFT correlation for weights h=excess(c), much faster O(n log n). Let's implement gradient of penalty over all lags via FFT correlation. This gives full subgradient for a differentiable penalty, better.

Compute h_s = φ'(c_s), e.g., h = (c > T) * (c - T) or top weights. Need L_h(w)_j = ∑_s h_s w_{s-j}. We can compute cross-correlation efficiently:
Let h_ext length M=2n-1. p length n. We need out[j]=∑_{i=0}^{n-1} p_i h_{i+j} where i+j < M. This is the "valid" correlation of h with p (no flip). In FFT: zero-pad p and h to N. Cross-correlation via ifft(F_p * conj(F_h)) gives correlation c_k = ∑ p[i] h[i+k] for k negative? Let's recall numpy `np.correlate(h, p, mode='full')` returns sum h[m+k] p[m] maybe. We can use `np.correlate(h, p, 'full')` but C direct O(M*n) not. FFT: `corr = np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(p,N) * np.conj(np.fft.fft(h,N))).real`. The output index k (0..N-1) corresponds to ∑_i p_i h_{i+k} with circular convolution? Let's derive: DFT of p, conj(DFT h) = DFT of circular correlation: r_k = ∑_n p_n h_{(n+k) mod N}. Thus if N≥M+n-1 no wrap, for k=0..n-1 (or N? ), r_k = ∑_{n=0}^{n-1} p_n h_{n+k} (if n+k<M) else h negative? h padded zero for >=M, but h index n+k can be up to 2n-2; N large. Good! So `out[j]` for j=0..n-1 is r_j. Perfect. Need r_j real. Use `r = np.fft.irfft?` We can do ifft full and take real first n. Since p,h real, result real. Use `np.fft.ifft(Fp*conj(Fh)).real[:n]`.
Check small n=2: p=[p0,p1], h=[h0,h1,h2]. N≥4. r0= p0h0+p1h1. But expected grad p0/2 = h0 p0 + h1 p1. Good. r1=p0h1+p1h2. expected p1: h1 p0 + h2 p1. Good. Great! So gradient for penalty P=∑φ(c_s) is 2*r.

Thus we can compute full gradient efficiently:
- c = conv(p,p) via rfft square.

```
def random_logpoly_search(n, X, size, rng, time_limit, init_list):
   best_score=inf; best_theta=None; best_w=None
   for start in init_list + randoms:
       theta=start.copy()
       sc=eval...
       if sc<best_score: store best
       # local coordinate descent for maybe 50s per start?
       steps = np.ones(d)*scale
       stall=0
       start_local=time.monotonic()
       while time<end_local and time<deadline:
          for k in range(d):
             for delta in (steps[k], -steps[k]):
                 trial=theta; trial[k]+=delta
                 trial_sc=eval
                 if trial_sc<theta_sc:
                    theta=trial; theta_sc=trial_sc; steps[k]*=1.1; stall=0
                    if theta_sc<best_score: update global
                 else:
                    steps[k]=max(1e-4, steps[k]*0.8)
          stall+=1
          if stall>200: break
       # if time left, new random start
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0,budget_s-5)
  n=8192
  size=1; while size<2*n-1: size*=2
  best_w=None; best_score=np.inf
  # geometric best
  for L in linspace(0.2,3,150): compute score and if better generate w? Actually generate w for best L.
  # parent resample if available
  # logpoly search allocate maybe 250s
  lp_deadline=min(deadline, start+250)
  best_theta, best_lp_score = logpoly_search(n,X,size,rng,lp_deadline, init_theta)
  if best_lp_score<best_score: generate w.
  # Also create random logpoly candidates and evaluate a few
  # gradient refine until deadline-5
  if time < deadline-5:
      best_w,best_score = gradient_refine(best_w, n,size,rng, deadline)
  # final safety: if best_score not finite, return ones
  return [float(x) for x in best_w]
```

```
def coordinate_one_theta(theta, X, n, size, rng, time_limit):
    d=len(theta)
    theta=theta.copy()
    sc=logpoly_objective(theta, X,n,size)
    if not finite: random
    steps=np.full(d,0.5)
    stall=0
    end=time_limit
    while time<end and stall<1000:
        changed=False
        for k in range(d):
            # adaptive step scale maybe based on abs(theta[k])+1
            step=steps[k]*max(1.0, abs(theta[k])*0.1)
            for delta in (step, -step):
                trial=theta.copy(); trial[k]+=delta

                   theta=trial; sc=tsc; steps[k]=min(10, steps[k]*1.15); changed=True; break
                else:
                   steps[k]=max(1e-3, steps[k]*0.75)
            if time>end: break
        if not changed:
            stall+=1
            # random kick
            theta = theta + rng.normal(0, 0.2, d); maybe clip
        else: stall=0
    return theta, sc
```

```
def spectral_refine(w, n, size, rng, time_limit, best_score):
   M=2n-1; best=...
   # current c
   for iter:
     c=conv(w)
     # shave level = min(best_c*0.99? or current*0.9)
     level = max(1e-12, 0.9*maxc or target?)

     # if sum too small? renormalize: r_target/=sum
     # Need PSD blend: R_t=fft(r_target); R0=fft(c); find alpha in [0,1] s.t. R_t + alpha*(R0-R_t) >= -eps. Binary search 20.
```

Top-level `spectral_update(w, n, size, level)`:
- c=conv(w); c=np.maximum(c,0); s=c.sum(); if s>0 c/=s.
- r=np.minimum(c, level); r_sum=r.sum(); if small return w; r/=r_sum.
- R=np.fft.fft(r, size); R=np.maximum(np.real(R),0)
- P=np.fft.fft(w, size); phase=np.angle(P)
- p=np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(R)*phase).real
- p = p[:n]
- p=np.clip(p,0,None)
- psum=p.sum(); if psum<=0 return w
- return p/psum
Note: `w` length n padded to size zeros; P phase has zeros where magnitude small. The spectral factor of shaved c may not be length n; projection discards rest. Good.
Then in refine, periodically apply spectral_update with level = best_c*0.98 or current*0.95, evaluate; if score improves accept.
This adds diversity. We can call every e.g. 100 gradient iterations or after stall. Since spectral_update itself FFTs: conv, fft r, fft w, ifft, score; ~4 FFTs. Okay.

```
def sequence_score(w, n, size):
    w=np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size != n: w = w[:n] if w.size>n else pad? Avoid.
    w=np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w=np.clip(w,0,1000.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s < 1e-300: return np.inf
    w=w/s
    W=np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(W*W, size)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cm=c.max()
    return float(2.0*n*cm)
```
For performance, in loops w already normalized finite; but okay. Could have `fast_score(w,n,size)` no clip to save. But use one.

`gradient_for_max(w,c,n,size)` computes h and correlation:
```
def gradient_max(w,c,n,size):
    maxc=c.max()
    if maxc <=0: return zeros,0
    scale=0.25*maxc
    # avoid overflow: (c-maxc)/scale <=0, exp<=1
    h1=np.exp((c-maxc)/scale)
    threshold=0.7*maxc
    h2=np.maximum(c-threshold,0.0)/maxc
    h=0.5*h1+0.5*h2
    h/=h.max() # optional
    Fw=np.fft.fft(w, size)
    Fh=np.fft.fft(h, size)
    grad=np.fft.ifft(Fw*np.conj(Fh)).real[:n]
    grad=np.maximum(grad,0) # should be nonnegative
    return grad, maxc
```
Use full fft because h length M<size, w length n. rfft could be used with complex? Full okay.

`logpoly_objective(theta,X,n,size)`:
- if theta length mismatch, return inf
- logw = theta @ X.T? X shape (n,d). Use `X @ theta`.
- finite check
- max, exp
- wsum

```
def logpoly_objective(theta, X, n, size):
   logw = X @ theta
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(logw)): return inf
   logw -= logw.max()
   w=np.exp(logw)
   s=w.sum()
   if s < 1e-300: return inf
   w /= s
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
   c=np.fft.irfft(W*W,size)[:2*n-1]
   return float(2*n*np.max(c))
```
Need handle if logw max -inf? no.

`make_logpoly_w(theta,X)` returns normalized w or None.

```
def best_geometric(n,size):
   log1=np.log(np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float))
   log2=np.log(np.arange(1,n,dtype=float)) # t=0..n-2? t+1 1..n-1
   bestL=1.0; bestScore=inf
   for L in np.linspace(0.15,5.0,400):
      logb=-L/n
      # logS = log1p(-exp(n logb)) - log1p(logb)
      exp_n = np.exp(n*logb)
      if exp_n >=1: continue
      logS = np.log1p(-exp_n) - np.log1p(logb)
      low = log1 + np.arange(n)*logb
      high = log2 + (2*n-2 - np.arange(n-1))*logb
      logM = max(low.max(), high.max())
      score=2*n*np.exp(logM - 2*logS)
      if score<bestScore: ...
   b=np.exp(bestL/n?) # L/n
   i=np.arange(n)
   w=np.exp(-bestL * i / n)
   w/=w.sum()
   return w, bestScore
```

Check log1p(logb) for logb negative small: 1-b = -expm1(logb). `np.log1p(logb)` equals log(1+logb) not log(1-b). Oops. Need log(1-b)=log(-expm1(logb)). Use `np.log(-np.expm1(logb))` stable. So logS = log1p(-exp(nlogb)) - log(-expm1(logb)). For L large exp(nlogb) small; log1p(-small) ok; for L small exp close1, log1p(-~1) may be -inf; handle if exp_n > 1-1e-15? return make_logpoly_w(best_theta, X), best_score

```
def coordinate_theta(theta, X,n,size,rng,time_limit):
  d=len(theta); theta=theta.copy()
  sc=logpoly_objective(theta,X,n,size)
  if not finite: theta=rng.normal... sc=...
  steps=np.ones(d)*0.5

  while time<time_limit and stall<500:
     for k in range(d):

        # scale by (1+abs(theta[k])?)

        else:
           trial_m=theta.copy(); trial_m[k]-=step
           sm=...

           else: step*=0.7; steps[k]=step
        if time>time_limit: break
     if stall... random kick
  return theta, sc
```

Active gradient refine top-level `gradient_refine(w,n,size,rng,start_time,deadline,best_score)`:
```
w=np.asarray(w,float).copy(); w=np.where(finite,0); w=np.clip(w,0,1000); s=sum; if s<eps return w,inf; w/=s
best=w.copy(); best_score=...
eta=0.2; stall=0; it=0; max_iter...?
while time<deadline:
   c=fft_conv_unit(w,n,size)
   maxc=c.max(); score=2*n*maxc
   if score<best_score:
       best_score=score; best=w.copy(); stall=0
   else:
       stall+=1
   # gradient
   maxc_safe=max(maxc,1e-300)
   h1=np.exp((c-maxc_safe)/(0.25*maxc_safe))
   h2=np.maximum(c-0.7*maxc_safe,0)/maxc_safe
   h=0.5*h1+0.5*h2
   hmax=h.max(); if hmax>0: h/=hmax
   Fw=np.fft.fft(w,size); Fh=np.fft.fft(h,size)
   grad=np.fft.ifft(Fw*np.conj(Fh)).real[:n]
   grad=np.maximum(grad,0)
   gm=grad.max()
   accepted=False
   if gm>1e-300:
       cand = w * np.exp(-eta*grad/gm)
       cs=cand.sum()
       if cs>1e-300:
           cand/=cs
           scand=fast_score(cand,n,size)
           if scand < score:
               w=cand; eta=min(2.0, eta*1.02); accepted=True; it=0
           else:
               eta=max(0.01, eta*0.7)
   # spectral escape
   if (not accepted and eta<0.05) or it%2000==0 or stall>3000:
       level = max(1e-9, min(0.9*maxc_safe, best_score/(2*n)*0.99))
       cand = spectral_update(w,n,size,level)
       if cand is not None:
           scand=fast_score(cand,n,size)
           if scand < score:
              w=cand; best if improves; eta=0.2; stall=0; accepted=True
           else:
              # perturb
              sigma = 0.1 + min(0.5, stall/2000)
              w = w*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
              w/=w.sum()
       else:
              w = w*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2,n)); w/=w.sum()
   # if big stall, reset to best and perturb
   if stall>10000:
       w=best.copy()
       w = w*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3,n)); w/=w.sum(); eta=0.2; stall=0
   it+=1
return best,best_score
```

`spectral_update(w,n,size,level)`:
- c=fft_conv_unit(w,n,size); c=np.maximum(c,0); csum=c.sum(); if csum small return None; c/=csum.
- r=np.minimum(c, level); rsum=r.sum(); if rsum<1e-12 return None; r/=rsum.
- R=np.fft.fft(r,size); R=np.maximum(np.real(R),0.0)
- P=np.fft.fft(w,size)
- phase=np.angle(P)
- p=np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(R)*phase).real
- p=p[:n]; p=np.clip(p,0,None); ps=p.sum(); if ps<1e-12 return None; p/=ps
- return p

Let's code `fast_score` top-level:
```
def fast_score(w,n,size):
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return inf
    if s != 1.0: w=w/s  # creates copy; in most calls s=1
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(W*W,size)[:2*n-1]
    cm=np.max(c)
    if cm<0: cm=0.0
    return float(2*n*cm)
```

Top lags gradient computed by correlation with one-hot: for lag s, gradient r = w_{s-j} slice; we can build h array at s=1. But we already have gradient via FFT for h one-hot same cost. Simpler: set h = zeros, h[max_idx]=1; maybe top2 add weights. Use FFT gradient. This might chase peak. Let's implement inside refine: `mode = rng.random()`; if <0.25 use top1 h; if <0.5 top10? But computing c already. For topK h:
```
if rng.random()<0.35:
  idx=np.argmax(c); h=np.zeros_like(c); h[idx]=1.0
else:
  # maybe top 20 by c
  top=np.argpartition(c, -20)[-20:]; h=zeros; h[top]=c[top]/(maxc)
```
But top20 h positive; using c values. Could be good. Let's use adaptive:
- 30% use single max (aggressive)
- 30% use top 16 weighted by excess
- 40% smooth h1/h2.
This adds diversity.
Need top 16: `idx=np.argpartition(c, -16)[-16:]`; h[idx]= (c[idx]/maxc)**2.
Single max: h[maxidx]=1.
Smooth as above.

Gradient via FFT each mode.

Step eta: For single max, eta maybe 1.0; smooth 0.2. We can set `eta_base = 1.0 if aggressive else 0.3`. But keep one adapted eta; maybe if mode aggressive multiply eta by 2 in trial? Use `eta_use = eta * (2.0 if aggressive else 1.0)`.

Line search: candidate if sc < score*0.999? use < score. Accept.

If no accept for many, perturb.

Time. Let's implement.

One more thing: The sequence length n=8192; `np.argpartition(c, -16)` O(M). okay.

Now final code. Ensure no type hints? okay. Use `np.inf`.

Let's produce final.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time

"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The code combines:
1. Analytic geometric/exponential seeds, which already beat the naive
   all-ones value 2.
2. Low-dimensional log-polynomial seed optimization.
3. High-dimensional multiplicative spectral/gradient refinement using the
   full max-convolution subdynamic and peak-shaving penalties.
4. Periodic spectral phase retention and random restarts to avoid local minima.
"""


def next_pow2(m):
    s = 1
    while s < m:
        s *= 2
    return s


def fft_conv_unit(w, n, size):
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(W * W, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    c[np.less(c, 0.0, out=c, where=(c < 0.0))] = 0.0
    return c


def fast_score(w, n, size):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    if s != 1.0:
        w = w / s
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(W * W, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm < 0.0:
        cm = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cm)


def make_logpoly_w(theta, X):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return None
    logw = X @ theta
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logw)):
        return None
    m = float(np.max(logw))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return None
    logw = logw - m
    w = np.exp(logw)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    w = w / s
    return w


def logpoly_objective(theta, X, n, size):
    w = make_logpoly_w(theta, X)
    if w is None:
        return np.inf
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(W * W, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm < 0.0:
        cm = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cm)


def coordinate_theta(theta, X, n, size, rng, time_limit):
    d = X.shape[1]
    theta = np.clip(np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy(), -60.0, 60.0)
    sc = logpoly_objective(theta, X, n, size)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        theta = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 2.0, d), -40.0, 40.0)
        sc = logpoly_objective(theta, X, n, size)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        theta = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
        theta[0] = -1.0
        sc = logpoly_objective(theta, X, n, size)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return theta, np.inf

    steps = np.full(d, 0.35, dtype=np.float64)
    stall = 0
    while time.monotonic() < time_limit and stall < 1200:
        improved_sweep = False
        for k in range(d):
            step = steps[k] * max(0.35, 1.0 + 0.05 * abs(theta[k]))
            # positive try
            trial = theta.copy()
            trial[k] += step
            trial_sc = logpoly_objective(trial, X, n, size)
            if np.isfinite(trial_sc) and trial_sc < sc:
                theta = trial
                sc = trial_sc
                steps[k] = min(8.0, steps[k] * 1.15)
                improved_sweep = True
                stall = 0
            else:
                trial = theta.copy()
                trial[k] -= step
                trial_sc = logpoly_objective(trial, X, n, size)
                if np.isfinite(trial_sc) and trial_sc < sc:
                    theta = trial
                    sc = trial_sc
                    steps[k] = min(8.0, steps[k] * 1.15)
                    improved_sweep = True
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    steps[k] = max(0.015, steps[k] * 0.78)
            if time.monotonic() >= time_limit:
                break

        if not improved_sweep:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 25 == 0:
                theta = np.clip(theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, d), -40.0, 40.0)
                sc = logpoly_objective(theta, X, n, size)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    theta = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 2.0, d), -40.0, 40.0)
        else:
            stall = 0
    return theta, sc


def logpoly_search(n, X, size, rng, deadline, init_thetas):
    d = X.shape[1]
    best_theta = None
    best_score = np.inf
    num_starts = 70
    for i in range(num_starts):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - now)
        rem_starts = max(1, num_starts - i)
        tper = min(9.0, max(1.2, rem / rem_starts))

        if i < len(init_thetas):
            theta = np.asarray(init_thetas[i], dtype=np.float64).copy()
        elif best_theta is not None and i > 0 and (i % 10 == 5):
            theta = np.clip(best_theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, d), -45.0, 45.0)
        else:
            theta = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 2.5, d), -45.0, 45.0)

        theta, sc = coordinate_theta(theta, X, n, size, rng, min(deadline, now + tper))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_theta = np.clip(np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy(), -60.0, 60.0)

    if best_theta is None:
        best_theta = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
        best_theta[0] = -1.0
        best_score = logpoly_objective(best_theta, X, n, size)

    w = make_logpoly_w(best_theta, X)
    if w is None:
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= w.sum()
        best_score = fast_score(w, n, size)
    return w, float(best_score)


def best_geometric(n, size):
    # Use logs to avoid overflow/underflow for large n.
    low_log_counts = np.log(np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64))
    high_log_counts = np.log(np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64))  # t=0..n-2

    bestL = 1.0
    bestsc = np.inf
    Ls = np.linspace(0.18, 4.5, 340)
    for L in Ls:
        logb = -float(L) / float(n)
        exp_n = np.exp(n * logb)
        if exp_n >= 1.0 - 1e-15:
            continue
        log_num = np.log1p(-exp_n)
        log_den = np.log(-np.expm1(logb))
        logS = log_num - log_den
        if not np.isfinite(logS):
            continue

        low_logs = low_log_counts + np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) * logb
        high_logs = high_log_counts + (2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)) * logb
        logM = max(float(np.max(low_logs)), float(np.max(high_logs)))
        if not np.isfinite(logM):
            continue
        sc = 2.0 * n * np.exp(logM - 2.0 * logS)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc:
            bestsc = float(sc)
            bestL = float(L)

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.exp(-bestL * (i / float(n)))
    w += 1e-300
    w /= w.sum()
    sc = fast_score(w, n, size)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        sc = 2.0
    return w, float(sc)


def spectral_update(w, n, size, level):
    c = fft_conv_unit(w, n, size)
    csum = float(np.sum(c))
    if not np.isfinite(csum) or csum <= 1e-300:
        return None
    c = c / csum

    r = np.minimum(c, max(1e-15, float(level)))
    rsum = float(np.sum(r))
    if not np.isfinite(rsum) or rsum <= 1e-300:
        return None
    r = r / rsum

    R = np.fft.fft(r, size)
    R = np.maximum(np.real(R), 0.0)

    P = np.fft.fft(w, size)
    phase = np.angle(P)

    p = np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(R) * phase).real
    p = p[:n]
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    ps = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return p / ps


def gradient_update_sortie(w, c, maxc, rng):
    """Make a non-negative pseudo-gradient vector h to reduce high-convolution lags."""
    M = c.size
    mode = rng.random()
    if mode < 0.30:
        h = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        h[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return h, True
    if mode < 0.60:
        k = min(24, M)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        h = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        vals = c[idx]
        vals = vals / max(maxc, 1e-300)
        h[idx] = vals * vals
        return h, True
    scale = 0.25 * maxc
    h1 = np.exp((c - maxc) / scale)
    threshold = 0.68 * maxc
    h2 = np.maximum(c - threshold, 0.0) / maxc
    h = 0.45 * h1 + 0.55 * h2
    hm = float(np.max(h))
    if hm > 0.0:
        h = h / hm
    return h, False


def gradient_refine(w, n, size, rng, deadline, best_score_in):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.clip(w, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        s = 1.0
    w = w / s

    best = w.copy()
    best_score = float(best_score_in)
    cur_score = fast_score(w, n, size)
    if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score:
        best_score = float(cur_score)
        best = w.copy()

    eta = 0.35
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    floor = 1e-18 / n

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = fft_conv_unit(w, n, size)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        score = fast_score(w, n, size)
        if maxc <= 0.0:
            maxc = 1e-300

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = float(score)
            best = w.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        h, aggressive = gradient_update_sortie(w, c, maxc, rng)
        use_eta = eta * (2.1 if aggressive else 1.0)

        accepted = False
        gm = 0.0
        Fw = None
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        if hmax > 0.0 and use_eta > 1e-12:
            hh = h / hmax
            Fw = np.fft.fft(w, size)
            Fh = np.fft.fft(hh, size)
            grad = np.fft.ifft(Fw * np.conj(Fh)).real[:n]
            grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
            grad = np.maximum(grad, 0.0)
            gm = float(np.max(grad))

        if gm > 1e-300:
            cand = w * np.exp(-use_eta * grad / gm)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
            cs = float(np.sum(cand))
            if np.isfinite(cs) and cs > 1e-300:
                cand = cand / cs
                cand_score = fast_score(cand, n, size)
                if np.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score < score:
                    w = cand
                    if aggressive:
                        eta = min(3.0, eta * 1.05)
                    else:
                        eta = min(2.0, eta * 1.03)
                    accepted = True
                else:
                    eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.72)

        if (not accepted and eta < 0.05) or (it > 0 and it % 1200 == 0):
            best_c = best_score / (2.0 * n)
            level = min(0.9 * maxc, max(1e-12, best_c * 1.001))
            cand = spectral_update(w, n, size, level)
            if cand is not None:
                cs = float(np.sum(cand))
                if cs > 1e-300:
                    cand = cand / cs
                    cscore = fast_score(cand, n, size)
                    if np.isfinite(cscore) and cscore < score:
                        w = cand
                        eta = 0.35
                        stall = 0
                        accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            # Stagnation: mild to moderate random multiplicative perturbation.
            sigma = 0.08 + 0.35 * min(1.5, float(stall) / 8000.0)
            w = w * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
            w = np.maximum(w, floor)
            ws = float(np.sum(w))
            if ws <= 1e-300:
                w = best.copy()
            else:
                w = w / ws

        if stall > 6000:
            w = best.copy()
            w = w * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, n))
            w = np.maximum(w, floor)
            ws = float(np.sum(w))
            if ws > 1e-300:
                w = w / ws
            else:
                w = best.copy()
            eta = 0.35
            stall = 0

        if it % 2500 == 0:
            supp = int(np.count_nonzero(w > 1e-12))
            if supp < max(64, n // 8):
                w = best.copy()
                w = w * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n))
                w = np.maximum(w, floor)
                ws = float(np.sum(w))
                if ws > 1e-300:
                    w = w / ws
                else:
                    w = best.copy()

        it += 1

    # Final exact FFT-based verification and cleanup.
    best = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64)
    bs = float(np.sum(best))
    if bs <= 1e-300:
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best_score = 2.0
    else:
        best = best / bs
        fs = fast_score(best, n, size)
        if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_score:
            best_score = float(fs)

    return best, float(best_score)


def normalize_nonneg(w, n):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size < n:
        pad = np.zeros(n - w.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w = np.concatenate((w, pad))
    else:
        w = w[:n]
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.clip(w, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return w / s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 12.0)

    # Fixed length chosen for a good tradeoff between attack surface and FFT cost.
    n = 8192
    M = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(M)

    best_w = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # 1) Geometric seed.
    geo_w, geo_sc = best_geometric(n, size)
    if np.isfinite(geo_sc) and geo_sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(geo_sc)
        best_w = normalize_nonneg(geo_w, n)
    else:
        best_w = normalize_nonneg(geo_w, n)
        best_score = fast_score(best_w, n, size)
    print(f"[seed geometric] score={best_score:.6f} (n={n})")

    # Optional parent resampling.  It is usually not better, but it is cheap.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(globals().get("height_sequence_1", None), dtype=np.float64)
        if parent is not None and parent.size > 10:
            parent = normalize_nonneg(parent, n)
            psc = fast_score(parent, n, size)
            if np.isfinite(psc) and psc < best_score:
                best_score = float(psc)
                best_w = parent
                print(f"[seed parent] score={best_score:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Low-dimensional log-polynomial seed search.
    X_init = (
        np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float64),
    )
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    X = np.column_stack(
        (
            x,
            x * x,
            x ** 3,
            x ** 4,
            x * (1.0 - x),
            (x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5),
        )
    )

    inits = []
    base = -1.0
    inits.append(base + np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float64))
    for a1 in (-4.0, -2.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        th = np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float64)
        th[0] = base
        th[1] = a1
        inits.append(th)
    for b2, b4 in (
        (3.0, -2.0),
        (-3.0, 2.0),
        (2.0, -4.0),
        (-2.0, 4.0),
        (5.0, -3.0),
        (-5.0, 3.0),
    ):
        th = np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float64)
        th[0] = -1.0
        th[1] = b2
        th[3] = b4
        inits.append(th)
    for _ in range(12):
        th = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 2.0, 6), -30.0, 30.0)
        th[0] += rng.normal(-1.5, 1.0)
        inits.append(th)

    lp_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 280.0)
    lp_deadline = max(lp_deadline, t0 + 20.0)
    lp_w, lp_sc = logpoly_search(n, X, size, rng, lp_deadline, inits)
    if np.isfinite(lp_sc) and lp_sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(lp_sc)
        best_w = normalize_nonneg(lp_w, n)
        print(f"[seed logpoly] score={best_score:.6f}")

    # 3) High-dimensional refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        best_w, best_score = gradient_refine(
            normalize_nonneg(best_w, n),
            n,
            size,
            rng,
            deadline,
            best_score,
        )
        print(f"[refined] score={best_score:.6f}")

    # Safety fallback.
    if best_w is None or best_score != best_score or best_score == np.inf:
        best_w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best_score = 2.0

    out = np.asarray(best_w, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    oss = float(np.sum(out))
    if oss <= 1e-300:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

    # Keep the value scale conventional and numerically pleasant.
    target_scale = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    out = out * (target_scale / max(1e-300, float(np.sum(out))))
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    # Optional final check against the authoritative evaluator if available.
    try:
        ev = evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in out])  # noqa: F821
        if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_score:
            best_score = float(ev)
            print(f"[evaluator] score={best_score:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(v) for v in out]
```
